I know there have been times I felt more like myself, but I can’t say exactly how long those stretches lasted.\n\nDoctor: Thinking back to that earlier time you mentioned feeling this way, do you recall if it lasted for at least two weeks or more?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure, honestly. I remember it being more than just a few bad days, but I can’t really say whether it was two weeks or longer.\n\nDoctor: When you do take a nap during the day, do you wake up feeling refreshed, or still just as tired as before?\n\nPatient: Still tired, really. The nap doesn’t seem to fix it. Even afterward, something small like unloading the dishwasher or trying to read for my certificate class can feel like too much.\n\nDoctor: On a typical night, about how many hours do you sleep in total?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure exactly. I haven’t noticed or kept track of whether it adds up to that much in a day."
